<a id="CPF-FR-005430"></a>
## CPF-FR-005430

- **Work Item / Priority:** `CPF-WP-CENTER-REPROCESS-03-VERIFICATION_EVIDENCE` / `P0`
- **Requirement / Acceptance:** [CENTER-REPROCESS / 기능 목적·업무 결과] failed-only·selected·range 재처리·approval·idempotency·prior result 보존·compensation·결과 비교를 제공한다. 이를 위해 해결할 업무 문제, 사용 역할, 미제공 위험과 완료 결과를 정의한다. 선언·Interface·DTO·문서만 추가하지 말고 실제 Consumer와 전체 호출 경로를 구현한다. | Batch 개발자·운영자가 실제 제품 경로에서 기능 목적·업무 결과 기능을 사용할 때 failed-only·selected·range 재처리·approval·idempotency·prior result 보존·compensation·결과 비교를 제공한다.의 결과와 상태·권한·Audit·복구 규칙이 일치해야 한다. Source/API/SQL/Test/Config/Frontend/Script와 exact-SHA Evidence가 연결돼야 한다.
- **Source / Default implementation:** cpf-batch/control-server/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/control/CenterCutReconciliationController.java; cpf-tools/db/vendor/{mariadb,postgresql,oracle}/runtime/bat/repository/centercut-reconcile-failed-*.sql; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/centercut/RemoteCpfCenterCutOperationsAdapter.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/controller/AdmCenterCutController.java | CENTER-REPROCESS product implementation traced at fc207ac5560da59f352ee0c5f83199177f2987b4
- **Consumer / Full call path:** Job·Scheduler·Worker·ADM | ADM approved center-cut operation -> control-server reconciliation controller | ADM reason/approval -> execution-scope reprocess API -> item transition -> execution transition -> audit -> scheduler/runner picks RETRY
- **Normal / Error / Partial failure:** Only failed items of one execution are requeued and execution counters transition together. | Zero-row/stale execution transition returns 409 and transaction rolls back. | invalid·401·403·404·409·429·timeout·dependency failure 표준 분류
- **UNKNOWN / Retry / Recovery / Reconcile:** Retry is execution-scoped and idempotency/approval evidence remains queryable. | side effect·DB commit·ACK/response loss 시 UNKNOWN 보존 후 대사 | retry·restart·reprocess·reconcile·compensation·rollback/forward recovery 중 적용
- **Idempotency / Concurrency / Security:** operationId+requestHash·CAS/lease/fencing 중 적용 | retry=안전한 단계만 bounded backoff+jitter; blind retry 금지 | Requester/approver separation, reason, approval request id and sanitized audit. | authN/authZ·trust boundary·Secret 외부화·secure default | Resource·Action·API·Field·Command 권한 Server 강제 | actor·target·before/after·reason·approval·result·transactionId append-only
- **DB·Message·Config / Frontend·OpenAPI:** Failed item/execution/audit SQL for three vendors; real DB transaction pending. | Owner DB/ledger/config store version·status·hash·audit 저장; 타 Owner DB 직접 갱신 금지 | DB 사용 시 MariaDB·PostgreSQL·Oracle lifecycle; DB-less 근거 | Backend contract ready; S05 generated client/execution-scope UI/E2E pending. | ADM/BZA 적용 시 구조화 UI·Server Paging·상태·권한·Audit·복구 | Public API/SPI·OpenAPI·Generated Client·Local/Remote 계약 일치
- **Test / Assertion:** PURPOSE Source trace + positive/negative test + 실제 Runtime/Browser/DB/Fault 적용 검증 | inventory=CenterCutReconciliationControllerTest; vendor SQL catalog tests; S02 behavior harness | CPF-FR-005430 [기능 목적·업무 결과/PURPOSE] acceptance를 기준으로 실제 Source와 Consumer 호출 경로가 존재하고, 정상·오류·복구·보안·DB/Frontend 영향이 CENTER-REPROCESS evidence에 개별 연결되어야 한다.
- **Command / Exit / Actual:** `GitHub Connector exact-SHA source/consumer inventory + local CSV contract reconstruction` / `0 (source/ledger reconstruction); target tools separated` / CPF-FR-005430 개별 판정: 완료/재확인 필요; Acceptance·Source·Consumer·호출경로·Assertion을 전용 Evidence anchor에 기록. 직접 Harness 적용 대상은 명령 결과를 연결했고 목표환경 차이는 재확인 필요.

- **Requirement / Acceptance:** [CENTER-REPROCESS / DB Rollback] failed-only·selected·range 재처리·approval·idempotency·prior result 보존·compensation·결과 비교를 제공한다. 이를 위해 Vendor별 rollback/forward recovery·backup checkpoint·재적용·부분 실패를 제공한다. 선언·Interface·DTO·문서만 추가하지 말고 실제 Consumer와 전체 호출 경로를 구현한다. | Batch 개발자·운영자가 실제 제품 경로에서 DB Rollback 기능을 사용할 때 failed-only·selected·range 재처리·approval·idempotency·prior result 보존·compensation·결과 비교를 제공한다.의 결과와 상태·권한·Audit·복구 규칙이 일치해야 한다. Source/API/SQL/Test/Config/Frontend/Script와 exact-SHA Evidence가 연결돼야 한다.
- **Source / Default implementation:** cpf-batch/control-server/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/control/CenterCutReconciliationController.java; cpf-tools/db/vendor/{mariadb,postgresql,oracle}/runtime/bat/repository/centercut-reconcile-failed-*.sql; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/centercut/RemoteCpfCenterCutOperationsAdapter.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/controller/AdmCenterCutController.java | CENTER-REPROCESS product implementation traced at fc207ac5560da59f352ee0c5f83199177f2987b4

- **Normal / Error / Partial failure:** Only failed items of one execution are requeued and execution counters transition together. | Zero-row/stale execution transition returns 409 and transaction rolls back. | invalid·401·403·404·409·429·timeout·dependency failure 표준 분류
- **UNKNOWN / Retry / Recovery / Reconcile:** Retry is execution-scoped and idempotency/approval evidence remains queryable. | side effect·DB commit·ACK/response loss 시 UNKNOWN 보존 후 대사 | retry·restart·reprocess·reconcile·compensation·rollback/forward recovery 중 적용
- **Idempotency / Concurrency / Security:** operationId+requestHash·CAS/lease/fencing 중 적용 | retry=안전한 단계만 bounded backoff+jitter; blind retry 금지 | Requester/approver separation, reason, approval request id and sanitized audit. | authN/authZ·trust boundary·Secret 외부화·secure default | Resource·Action·API·Field·Command 권한 Server 강제 | actor·target·before/after·reason·approval·result·transactionId append-only

- **Test / Assertion:** DB_ROLLBACK Source trace + positive/negative test + 실제 Runtime/Browser/DB/Fault 적용 검증 | inventory=CenterCutReconciliationControllerTest; vendor SQL catalog tests; S02 behavior harness | CPF-FR-005474 [DB Rollback/DB_ROLLBACK] acceptance를 기준으로 실제 Source와 Consumer 호출 경로가 존재하고, 정상·오류·복구·보안·DB/Frontend 영향이 CENTER-REPROCESS evidence에 개별 연결되어야 한다.

- **Requirement / Acceptance:** [CENTER-REPROCESS / BZA 업무 관리] failed-only·selected·range 재처리·approval·idempotency·prior result 보존·compensation·결과 비교를 제공한다. 이를 위해 업무·조직·결재·권한 관리가 필요한 경우 BZA Public Contract와 연결한다. 선언·Interface·DTO·문서만 추가하지 말고 실제 Consumer와 전체 호출 경로를 구현한다. | Batch 개발자·운영자가 실제 제품 경로에서 BZA 업무 관리 기능을 사용할 때 failed-only·selected·range 재처리·approval·idempotency·prior result 보존·compensation·결과 비교를 제공한다.의 결과와 상태·권한·Audit·복구 규칙이 일치해야 한다. Source/API/SQL/Test/Config/Frontend/Script와 exact-SHA Evidence가 연결돼야 한다.
- **Source / Default implementation:** cpf-batch/control-server/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/control/CenterCutReconciliationController.java; cpf-tools/db/vendor/{mariadb,postgresql,oracle}/runtime/bat/repository/centercut-reconcile-failed-*.sql; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/centercut/RemoteCpfCenterCutOperationsAdapter.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/controller/AdmCenterCutController.java | CENTER-REPROCESS product implementation traced at fc207ac5560da59f352ee0c5f83199177f2987b4
- **Consumer / Full call path:** Job·Scheduler·Worker·ADM | ADM approved center-cut operation -> control-server reconciliation controller | ADM reason/approval -> execution-scope reprocess API -> item transition -> execution transition -> audit -> scheduler/runner picks RETRY
- **Normal / Error / Partial failure:** Only failed items of one execution are requeued and execution counters transition together. | Zero-row/stale execution transition returns 409 and transaction rolls back. | invalid·401·403·404·409·429·timeout·dependency failure 표준 분류
- **UNKNOWN / Retry / Recovery / Reconcile:** Retry is execution-scoped and idempotency/approval evidence remains queryable. | side effect·DB commit·ACK/response loss 시 UNKNOWN 보존 후 대사 | retry·restart·reprocess·reconcile·compensation·rollback/forward recovery 중 적용
- **Idempotency / Concurrency / Security:** operationId+requestHash·CAS/lease/fencing 중 적용 | retry=안전한 단계만 bounded backoff+jitter; blind retry 금지 | Requester/approver separation, reason, approval request id and sanitized audit. | authN/authZ·trust boundary·Secret 외부화·secure default | Resource·Action·API·Field·Command 권한 Server 강제 | actor·target·before/after·reason·approval·result·transactionId append-only
- **DB·Message·Config / Frontend·OpenAPI:** Failed item/execution/audit SQL for three vendors; real DB transaction pending. | Owner DB/ledger/config store version·status·hash·audit 저장; 타 Owner DB 직접 갱신 금지 | DB 사용 시 MariaDB·PostgreSQL·Oracle lifecycle; DB-less 근거 | Backend contract ready; S05 generated client/execution-scope UI/E2E pending. | ADM/BZA 적용 시 구조화 UI·Server Paging·상태·권한·Audit·복구 | Public API/SPI·OpenAPI·Generated Client·Local/Remote 계약 일치
- **Test / Assertion:** BZA_UI Source trace + positive/negative test + 실제 Runtime/Browser/DB/Fault 적용 검증 | inventory=CenterCutReconciliationControllerTest; vendor SQL catalog tests; S02 behavior harness | CPF-FR-005479 [BZA 업무 관리/BZA_UI] acceptance를 기준으로 실제 Source와 Consumer 호출 경로가 존재하고, 정상·오류·복구·보안·DB/Frontend 영향이 CENTER-REPROCESS evidence에 개별 연결되어야 한다.

- **Requirement / Acceptance:** [CENTER-REPROCESS / Unit Test] failed-only·selected·range 재처리·approval·idempotency·prior result 보존·compensation·결과 비교를 제공한다. 이를 위해 순수 로직·validation·state transition·error mapping을 결정적으로 검증한다. 선언·Interface·DTO·문서만 추가하지 말고 실제 Consumer와 전체 호출 경로를 구현한다. | Batch 개발자·운영자가 실제 제품 경로에서 Unit Test 기능을 사용할 때 failed-only·selected·range 재처리·approval·idempotency·prior result 보존·compensation·결과 비교를 제공한다.의 결과와 상태·권한·Audit·복구 규칙이 일치해야 한다. Source/API/SQL/Test/Config/Frontend/Script와 exact-SHA Evidence가 연결돼야 한다.
- **Source / Default implementation:** cpf-batch/control-server/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/control/CenterCutReconciliationController.java; cpf-tools/db/vendor/{mariadb,postgresql,oracle}/runtime/bat/repository/centercut-reconcile-failed-*.sql; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/centercut/RemoteCpfCenterCutOperationsAdapter.java; cpf-admin/src/main/java/com/cpf/admin/opr/controller/AdmCenterCutController.java | CENTER-REPROCESS product implementation traced at fc207ac5560da59f352ee0c5f83199177f2987b4

- **Normal / Error / Partial failure:** Only failed items of one execution are requeued and execution counters transition together. | Zero-row/stale execution transition returns 409 and transaction rolls back. | invalid·401·403·404·409·429·timeout·dependency failure 표준 분류
- **UNKNOWN / Retry / Recovery / Reconcile:** Retry is execution-scoped and idempotency/approval evidence remains queryable. | side effect·DB commit·ACK/response loss 시 UNKNOWN 보존 후 대사 | retry·restart·reprocess·reconcile·compensation·rollback/forward recovery 중 적용

- **Test / Assertion:** UNIT_TEST Source trace + positive/negative test + 실제 Runtime/Browser/DB/Fault 적용 검증 | inventory=CenterCutReconciliationControllerTest; vendor SQL catalog tests; S02 behavior harness | CPF-FR-005484 [Unit Test/UNIT_TEST] acceptance를 기준으로 실제 Source와 Consumer 호출 경로가 존재하고, 정상·오류·복구·보안·DB/Frontend 영향이 CENTER-REPROCESS evidence에 개별 연결되어야 한다.
